## Lines

### f8962.forms — Number of Forms 1095-A
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"collection": "f1095a", "field": "f1095a.one", "op": "sum_over", "where": null}
```

Second:

```json
{"collection": "f1095a", "field": "f1095a.one", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"const": false}, {"ref": "f1095a.void"}], "test": "eq"}}
```

Ruling: scope — the candidate leaves out Forms 1095-A marked VOID, while the project counts every entered form and refuses the return at f8962.void_form (i8962 p. 2, "Do not use the information on the Form 1095-A with the “VOID” box checked or the previously received Form 1095-A to complete Form 8962."); that limb changes values only on stage18.refuse.void_1095a, which the project refuses. Triage executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/f8962.json`, rerun 2026-11-03: 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 128 partial records from 40 bases): 2,120 of 2,123 comparisons agree; differences by kind {"candidate-unknown": 2, "value": 1}; on completed returns none. No completed-return difference.

Second transcription's note: Tax family is you and your spouse (if filing jointly) unless someone can claim you, plus the dependents you claim. If you or your spouse can be claimed as a dependent, you cannot take the PTC and the person who claims you files Form 8962. That case is refused on sa.dependent. Married filing separately requires an exception that this tool does not support, so it is refused through f8962.h_mfs.

Ruling: interface gap — the candidate's line has no shown_when, so where the project hides it (no Form 1095-A, Form 8962 not completed, or the annual or monthly route not taken: i8962 p. 15, "If you checked “Yes” on line 10 and you are completing line 11, do not complete lines 12 through 23."; i8962 p. 16, "If you checked “No” on line 10 and you are completing lines 12 through 23, do not complete line 11.") it shows an amount or needs answers the hidden line never asks; the request labels shown_when Optional (T-163), as ruled in earlier rounds; where both show the line the expression is the project's. Triage executable-triage-v2 (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/f8962.json`, rerun 2026-11-03: 1,495 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 128 partial records from 40 bases): 200 of 2,123 comparisons agree; differences by kind {"candidate-refuses": 283, "candidate-shows-skipped": 1503, "candidate-unknown": 24, "project-unknown": 113}; on completed returns {"candidate-shows-skipped": 337}. The refusal-path differences are on returns the project or the candidate refuses: project-refuses where the project refuses at its own cited gates (f8962.alloc, f8962.marriage, f8962.void_form, f8962.premium_adjust, f8962.alien_branch, f8962.two_counted, dependent screens) or by a refusal reaching the line from another form, and candidate-refuses on the candidate's own declarations (for example sa.excluded_income and sa.dependent, read on records where the project hides the line); none is a completed-return amount. With the witness cap lifted (scratch), all 337 completed-return differences were listed: 337 are on records where the project hides the line; 0 where the project shows the line. No completed-return value difference.

lines:

  - id: f8962.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Total PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (e), or add lines 12 through 23, column (e), and enter the total here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}
          then: f8962.line11e
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}
          then: f8962.h_monthly_e_total
    refuses: [f8962.other]
    note: >-
      The text does not say directly which source line 24 uses. The instructions for line 10 decide it:
      "Yes" means complete line 11 and skip lines 12-23, and "No" means complete lines 12-23. So line 24
      reads line 11(e) when line 10 is true and the total of 12(e)-23(e) when it is false.
      The instructions also say to enter -0- on line 24 in some cases.
      (1) All enrolled family members are not lawfully present. That case is covered by the f8962.other
      screening input, so this line refuses on it rather than writing it as a zero.
      (2) Not an applicable taxpayer (married filing separately without Exception 2, or household income
      below 100% of the poverty line without an exception). Here the instructions skip columns (a)-(e).
      This transcription assumes those column (e) lines, reviewed separately, are then zero. It does not
      add its own override.
  - id: f8962.h_monthly_f_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add lines 12 through 23, column (f)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of:
        - f8962.line12f
        - f8962.line13f
        - f8962.line14f
        - f8962.line15f
        - f8962.line16f
        - f8962.line17f
        - f8962.line18f
        - f8962.line19f
        - f8962.line20f
        - f8962.line21f
        - f8962.line22f
        - f8962.line23f
  - id: f8962.line25
    printed: "25"
    label: "Advance payment of PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (f), or add lines 12 through 23, column (f), and enter the total here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}
          then: f8962.line11f
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}
          then: f8962.h_monthly_f_total
    note: >-
      Line 25 uses the same reading as line 24. When line 10 is "Yes" (line 11 completed), it takes
      line 11(f). Otherwise it takes the total of lines 12(f) through 23(f).
```
```

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f8962.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount (an entry of -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: f8962.h_mfs
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing separately"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: ["f1040.status", {const: "mfs"}]}

  - id: f8962.h_persons
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You, and your spouse if filing jointly"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.one}

  - id: f8962.h_deps
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Dependents you claim on your 2025 return"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: ["dependent.claimed", {const: true}]}

  - id: f8962.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Tax family size. Enter your tax family size."
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["f8962.h_persons", "f8962.h_deps"]}
    refuses: [sa.dependent, f8962.h_mfs]
    note: "Tax family is you and your spouse (if filing jointly) unless someone can claim you, plus the dependents you claim. If you or your spouse can be claimed as a dependent, you cannot take the PTC and the person who claims you files Form 8962. That case is refused on sa.dependent. Married filing separately requires an exception that this tool does not support, so it is refused through f8962.h_mfs."

  - id: f8962.h_ss_nontax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet 1-1, line 4: if line 6a is more than line 6b, subtract line 6b from line 6a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f1040.line6a", take: "f1040.line6b"}

  - id: f8962.line2a
    printed: "2a"
    label: "Modified AGI. Enter your modified AGI (Worksheet 1-1, line 5: AGI plus tax-exempt interest plus Form 2555 amounts plus nontaxable social security)."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["f1040.line11a", "f1040.line2a", "f8962.h_ss_nontax"]}
    refuses: [sa.excluded_income]
    note: "Form 2555 amounts (Worksheet 1-1, line 3) are not computed. A filer with Form 2555 is refused through sa.excluded_income. The Form 8814 adjustment is assumed to be already excluded by scope.other_line16."

  - id: f8962.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Enter the total of your dependents' modified AGI (only dependents required to file an income tax return)."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.ptc_magi
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: ["dependent.claimed", {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["dependent.ptc_files", {const: true}]}
    note: "This sums the input dependent.ptc_magi for claimed dependents required to file. I did not use dependent.ptc_magi_counted because its computation is not given."

  - id: f8962.h_3_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add the amounts on lines 2a and 2b, even if one or both are negative"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["f8962.line2a", "f8962.line2b"]}

  - id: f8962.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Household income. Add the amounts on lines 2a and 2b. If the total is less than zero, enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f8962.h_3_sum", take: {const: 0}}

  - id: f8962.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Federal poverty line. Enter the federal poverty line amount from Table 1-1, 1-2, or 1-3."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: ptc_poverty_line, keys: ["f8962.fpl_table", "f8962.line1"]}
    note: "This assumes the pinned table carries the per-person additions for family sizes over 8. A size with no row leaves the line unknown."

  - id: f8962.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Household income as a percentage of federal poverty line (Worksheet 2: if line 3 is more than 4.0 times line 4, enter 401; otherwise divide, multiply by 100 and drop decimals)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: percent_floor, part: "f8962.line3", whole: "f8962.line4", cap: 400}

  - id: f8962.line7
    printed: "7"
    label: "Applicable figure. Using your line 5 percentage, locate your applicable figure on the table in the instructions."
    type: Rate
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: ptc_applicable_figure, keys: ["f8962.line5"]}
    note: "This assumes Table 2 is pinned for every percentage from 0 to 401. Below 150 the figure is 0.0000; at 400 or more it is 0.0850. When household income is below 100% and neither exception applies, the line is still computed but the credit is set to zero at line 24."

  - id: f8962.line8a
    printed: "8a"
    label: "Annual contribution amount. Multiply line 3 by line 7. Round to nearest whole dollar amount."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: "f8962.line3", rate: "f8962.line7", unit: 100}

  - id: f8962.line8b
    printed: "8b"
    label: "Monthly contribution amount. Divide line 8a by 12. Round to nearest whole dollar amount."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: "f8962.line8a", rate: {param: ptc.twelfth}, unit: 100}

  - id: f8962.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Are you allocating policy amounts with another taxpayer or do you want to use the alternative calculation for year of marriage?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: ["f8962.alloc", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["f8962.married_in_2025", {const: true}]}
    refuses: [f8962.alloc, f8962.married_in_2025]
    note: "Parts IV and V are not supported. Any allocation situation, or a marriage in 2025 with coverage before the first full month of marriage, is refused, so the computed path always has line 9 = No."

  - id: f8962.forms
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms 1095-A used (not VOID and not superseded)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f1095a
      field: f1095a.one
      where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}

  - id: f8962.any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "At least one Form 1095-A is used"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: ["f8962.forms", {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "Read as: Form 8962 is needed because Marketplace coverage was reported on at least one Form 1095-A in use."

  - id: f8962.h_nonuniform
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Forms 1095-A without 12 months of coverage and unchanged columns A and B"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f1095a
      field: f1095a.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.uniform", {const: false}]}

  - id: f8962.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "See the instructions to determine if you can use line 11 or must complete lines 12 through 23 (Yes: full-year coverage with no changes on Form 1095-A, Part III, column A or B)."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: ["f8962.any", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["f8962.h_nonuniform", {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: f8962.line11a
    printed: "11(a)"
    label: "Annual enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f1095a
      field: f1095a.line33a
      where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}

  - id: f8962.line11b
    printed: "11(b)"
    label: "Annual applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f1095a
      field: f1095a.line33b
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.b_counted", {const: true}]}

  - id: f8962.line11c
    printed: "11(c)"
    label: "Annual contribution amount (line 8a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "f8962.line8a"}

  - id: f8962.line11d
    printed: "11(d)"
    label: "Annual maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f8962.line11b", take: "f8962.line11c"}

  - id: f8962.line11e
    printed: "11(e)"
    label: "Annual PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: ["f8962.line11a", "f8962.line11d"]}

  - id: f8962.line11f
    printed: "11(f)"
    label: "Annual advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f1095a
      field: f1095a.line33c
      where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}

  # ---------- January (line 12) ----------

    printed: "12(a)"

    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f8962.line18b", take: "f8962.line18c"}

    printed: "18(e)"
    label: "July: monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"

  # ---------- August (line 19) ----------
  - id: f8962.line19a

    printed: "20(e)"
    label: "September: monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: ["f8962.line20a", "f8962.line20d"]}
  - id: f8962.line20f
    printed: "20(f)"
    label: "September: monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, line 29, column C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.in_c_sep, where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}}

  # ---------- October (line 21) ----------
  - id: f8962.line21a
    printed: "21(a)"
    label: "October: monthly enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, line 30, column A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.a_oct, where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}}
  - id: f8962.line21b
    printed: "21(b)"
    label: "October: monthly applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, line 30, column B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.b_oct, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.b_counted", {const: true}]}]}}
  - id: f8962.line21c
    printed: "21(c)"
    label: "October: monthly contribution amount (amount from line 8b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "f8962.line8b"}
  - id: f8962.line21d
    printed: "21(d)"
    label: "October: monthly maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars

    printed: "21(e)"

  # ---------- November (line 22) ----------
  - id: f8962.line22a
    printed: "22(a)"
    label: "November: monthly enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, line 31, column A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.a_nov, where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}}
  - id: f8962.line22b
    printed: "22(b)"
    label: "November: monthly applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, line 31, column B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.b_nov, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.b_counted", {const: true}]}]}}
  - id: f8962.line22c
    printed: "22(c)"

  - id: f8962.line22d

    label: "November: monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, line 31, column C)"

  # ---------- December (line 23) ----------
  - id: f8962.line23a
    printed: "23(a)"
    label: "December: monthly enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, line 32, column A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.a_dec, where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}}
  - id: f8962.line23b
    printed: "23(b)"
    label: "December: monthly applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, line 32, column B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.b_dec, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.b_counted", {const: true}]}]}}
  - id: f8962.line23c
    printed: "23(c)"
    label: "December: monthly contribution amount (amount from line 8b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: "f8962.line8b"}
  - id: f8962.line23d
    printed: "23(d)"
    label: "December: monthly maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f8962.line23b", take: "f8962.line23c"}
  - id: f8962.line23e
    printed: "23(e)"
    label: "December: monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: ["f8962.line23a", "f8962.line23d"]}
  - id: f8962.line23f
    printed: "23(f)"
    label: "December: monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, line 32, column C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.in_c_dec, where: {op: test, eq: ["f1095a.void", {const: false}]}}

  - id: f8962.h_24_monthly
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add lines 12 through 23, column (e)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["f8962.line12e", "f8962.line13e", "f8962.line14e", "f8962.line15e", "f8962.line16e", "f8962.line17e", "f8962.line18e", "f8962.line19e", "f8962.line20e", "f8962.line21e", "f8962.line22e", "f8962.line23e"]}

  - id: f8962.h_25_monthly
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add lines 12 through 23, column (f)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: ["f8962.line12f", "f8962.line13f", "f8962.line14f", "f8962.line15f", "f8962.line16f", "f8962.line17f", "f8962.line18f", "f8962.line19f", "f8962.line20f", "f8962.line21f", "f8962.line22f", "f8962.line23f"]}

  - id: f8962.line25
    printed: "25"
    label: "Advance payment of PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (f), or add lines 12 through 23, column (f), and enter the total here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: ["f8962.line10", {const: true}]}, then: "f8962.line11f"}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: ["f8962.line10", {const: false}]}, then: "f8962.h_25_monthly"}
    refuses: [f8962.other]

  - id: f8962.h_aptc_paid
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "APTC was paid for the coverage of 1 or more months during 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: ["f8962.line25", {const: 0}]}

  - id: f8962.h_applicable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Applicable taxpayer: line 5 is at least 100, or the estimated-household-income or lawfully-present-alien exception applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, ge: ["f8962.line5", {const: 100, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: ["f8962.estimate_100", {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: ["f8962.h_aptc_paid", {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: ["f8962.alien_below", {const: true}]}
    note: "Below 100% without an exception you are not an applicable taxpayer. If APTC was paid, lines 7-8 are skipped and you go to line 9; this is carried as a PTC of zero on line 24. If no APTC was paid, the IRS says stop, and lines 24-29 are all zero."

  - id: f8962.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Total PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (e), or add lines 12 through 23, column (e), and enter the total here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: ["f8962.h_applicable", {const: false}]}
          then: "f8962.h_zero"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: ["f8962.h_applicable", {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: ["f8962.line10", {const: true}]}]}
          then: "f8962.line11e"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: ["f8962.h_applicable", {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: ["f8962.line10", {const: false}]}]}
          then: "f8962.h_24_monthly"
    refuses: [f8962.other, f8962.premium_adjust, f8962.same_state_counted]
    note: "Not lawfully present enrollees, other MEC, HRAs, unpaid premiums and similar cases are refused through f8962.other. Mid-month starts and premium refunds are refused through f8962.premium_adjust. Overlapping same-state column B entries are refused through f8962.same_state_counted."

  - id: f8962.line26
    printed: "26"
    label: "Net PTC. If line 24 is greater than line 25, subtract line 25 from line 24. Enter the difference here and on Schedule 3 (Form 1040), line 9. If line 24 equals line 25, enter -0-. If line 25 is greater than line 24, leave this line blank and continue to line 27."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f8962.line24", take: "f8962.line25"}
    note: "This line is held as zero when it is to be left blank (line 25 greater than line 24)."

  - id: f8962.line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Excess advance payment of PTC. If line 25 is greater than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 25. Enter the difference here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: "f8962.line25", take: "f8962.line24"}

  - id: f8962.h_r200
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Table 5: line 5 less than 200 ($375 single; $750 any other filing status)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_200}

  - id: f8962.h_r300
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Table 5: line 5 at least 200 but less than 300 ($975 single; $1,950 any other filing status)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_300}

  - id: f8962.h_r400
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Table 5: line 5 at least 300 but less than 400 ($1,625 single; $3,250 any other filing status)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_400}

  - id: f8962.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Repayment limitation (Table 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, lt: ["f8962.line5", {const: 200, type: Count}]}
          then: "f8962.h_r200"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: ["f8962.line5", {const: 200, type: Count}]}, {op: test, lt: ["f8962.line5", {const: 300, type: Count}]}]}
          then: "f8962.h_r300"
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: ["f8962.line5", {const: 300, type: Count}]}, {op: test, lt: ["f8962.line5", {const: 400, type: Count}]}]}
          then: "f8962.h_r400"
        - guard: {op: test, ge: ["f8962.line5", {const: 400, type: Count}]}
          then: "f8962.h_zero"
    note: "When line 5 is 400 or more, line 28 is left blank; it is held as zero and line 29 takes line 27 directly. The self-employed health insurance case is assumed to be refused through sc.hi_screen."

  - id: f8962.h_29_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 27 or line 28"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: ["f8962.line27", "f8962.line28"]}

  - id: f8962.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Excess advance PTC repayment. Enter the smaller of line 27 or line 28 here and on Schedule 2 (Form 1040), line 1a. If line 28 is blank, enter the amount from line 27."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, ge: ["f8962.line5", {const: 400, type: Count}]}
          then: "f8962.line27"
        - guard: {op: test, lt: ["f8962.line5", {const: 400, type: Count}]}
          then: "f8962.h_29_min"
